Let's implement efficient binary local search:
Represent h as int8 array 0/1 length n. Maintain C as int array length 2n-1 (counts) from np.correlate(h, 1-h) maybe int. For local search, accept moves reducing max_count or sum of high counts.

Candidate generation ideas:
1. For current max M and maybe all lags with C>=M-1 (or threshold), compute for each such lag a few promising swaps. Use A/B arrays and choose pairs with low Δ for that lag. Then full-evaluate exact delta (vectorized) and accept if new_max < M (or if new_max = M but score improves).
2. Use randomness: random swaps, if new_max ≤ M maybe accept; some with increase gradually (simulated annealing). Need keep within time.

Need exact delta computation efficient:
Given h int 0/1, C int counts length L=2n-1, lag offset off=n-1. Candidate swap: i (0->1), j (1->0). We can compute new_h maybe but delta vector using shifted h (old) as previous formula:
delta = zeros(L)
For lags ℓ = -n+1..n-1 stored idx=off+ℓ.
For i: add to idx where 0≤i+ℓ<n: +(1-h[i+ℓ])
sub where 0≤i-ℓ<n: -h[i-ℓ]
For j: sub where 0≤j+ℓ<n: -(1-h[j+ℓ])
add where 0≤j-ℓ<n: +h[j-ℓ]
Cross:
idx0=off: -2
idx1=off + (j-i): +1 (if within 0..n-1? since i≠j, idx in range)
idx2=off + (i-j): +1
Does this work for fractional? For binary yes. But if h values not binary, delta formula is not valid? We use binary search on binary h. Good.
We can vectorize: Let lap, lap_pos arrays? For a fixed i, im = i + arange; but allocating delta zeros each candidate length L is O(n) and memory. For many candidates maybe okay if n=500 and 10k candidates =>5e6, fine. But Python loop candidate with vector ops overhead. We can precompute shifted arrays? For exact candidate, we can compute new_h and full C? np.correlate O(n^2) too slow. Delta vector O(n) better.

Can optimize delta evaluation for small n using flat arrays and precomputed index slices? Precompute for each position p arrays of lags where p+ℓ valid? But each candidate needs mask depending p. Could precompute for each p:
- idx_plus[p] = off + (range? ℓ such that p+ℓ valid) = off + (0..n-1-p) for ℓ from 0 to n-1-p. Then h[p+ℓ] = h[p:]+? For i add (1 - h[i:p?]). Specifically ℓ = b-i for b from i to n-1 => idx = off + (b-i) = off + b - i. Values 1-h[b]. So contiguous slice in h: h[i:n]. idx range off to off+n-1-i.
- i second: a from 0 to i, ℓ=i-a, idx = off + i - a = off+i-a; values h[a]. contig idx off+i down to off? Could use reversed.
For j similarly.
Thus delta can be built with contiguous slices:
delta = zeros(L)
# i first (i zero): b=i..n-1
delta[off : off + (n-i)] += 1 - h[i:n]
# i second: a=0..i
delta[off - i : off + 1] -= h[0:i+1] reversed? Lag ℓ=i-a; idx=off+i-a. For a=0 -> off+i; a=i->off. So indices off:off+i+1 correspond a=i..0? We can do delta[off:i+1? off+i?]. Let's be precise: idx = off + (i-a). As a runs 0..i, idx runs off+i down to off. The values h[a] need match idx. Could assign delta[off:off+i+1] -= h[i::-1]? Let idx ascending off..off+i corresponds a=i..0. h[a] descending: h[i.. -1], so yes delta[off:off+i+1] -= h[i::-1].
# j first (j one): b=j..n-1, subtract 1 - h[j:n]
delta[off : off+n-j] -= 1 - h[j:n] (old h[j]=1, but at b=j value 0, ok)
# j second: a=0..j, add h[a]
idx ascending off..off+j => a=j..0, so delta[off:off+j+1] += h[j::-1]
# self/cross corrections as above.
This uses contiguous slice additions, fast.
Check signs: previous formula i second subtract h[imn] where imn=i-ℓ=a; values h[a] at idx off+i-a; yes.
Cross corrections: delta[off]-=2; delta[off+j-i]+=1; delta[off+i-j]+=1. For i zero, j one. For i and j adjacent? good.
Then newC = C + delta, newmax = newC.max(). Need ensure newC nonnegative? For binary exact should. Could use int16? Counts up to n ~1000, int16 ok. L ~1999. For speed use int32.
Candidate generation per high lag can compute A_i and B_j arrays with scalar loops or vector slices.

if i+ℓ in [0,n): (1 - h[i+ℓ]) else 0
minus if i-ℓ in [0,n): h[i-ℓ] else 0.
Because ℓ can be negative. We can generate for all i vectorized:
baseA = zeros(n)
if ℓ>=0:
 range_i_first: i=0..n-1-ℓ -> baseA[i] += 1 - h[i+ℓ]
 range_i_second: i=ℓ..n-1? i-ℓ>=0 -> baseA[i] -= h[i-ℓ]
else: let d=-ℓ>0.
 i+ℓ = i-d valid i=d..n-1 -> baseA[i] += 1 - h[i-d]
 i-ℓ = i+d valid i=0..n-d-1 -> baseA[i] -= h[i+d]
Similarly B_j =
- if j+ℓ valid: 1-h[j+ℓ]
+ if j-ℓ valid: h[j-ℓ]
This is A_i and B_j but sign for B is negative first plus second. So B = -first + second. We can compute by same first/second with opposite sign.

def binary_local_search(h, C, M, seconds, n_restarts, seed):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    best_h = h.copy(); best_C=C.copy(); best_M=M; best_score=score(C)
    for restart in range(...):
       h=current restart
       M=current
       no_iter=0
       while time<deadline:
          candidates = generate_candidates(h,C,M,limit, rng)
          improved=False
          for (i,j) in candidates unique:
             newC, newM = eval_swap(C, h, i,j)
             if newM < M or (newM==M and score(newC)<score(C)):
                apply; improved=True; break
          if not improved:
             # try random candidates maybe with anneal accept
             for random swaps up to R:
                 newC,newM=eval
                 if newM <= M+? and condition accept; maybe if newM<M accept; if newM==M and score< accept; if anneal accept newM=M+1 with prob
             if no improve: break / perturb
       update best
```

For B_j = -first_j + second_j = -pos[j] + neg[j] if same valid formula? Wait first term for j: 1-h[j+ℓ] (valid), second h[j-ℓ] (valid). So B = -pos[j] + neg[j]. Good.
Compute ranges based on ℓ:
If ℓ>=0:
  pos[0:n-ℓ] = 1 - h[ℓ:n]
  neg[ℓ:n] = h[0:n-ℓ]
If ℓ<0, d=-ℓ:
  pos[d:n] = 1 - h[0:n-d]
  neg[0:n-d] = h[d:n]
Then zeros_mask = h==0, ones_mask = h==1.

def generate_candidates(h,C,M,n,off,limit,rng):
    cand=[]
    # top idx: np.argsort(C)[-k]? But top values same M. Use where C==M; if too many, sample.
    top = np.where(C == M)[0]
    if len(top)>60: top = rng.choice(top,60,replace=False)
    # also include M-1 maybe if countM large? no.
    for idx in top:
       ell = idx-off
       pos,neg = compute_pos_neg(h,ell,n)
       A = pos - neg
       B = -pos + neg
       # positions zeros/ones
       zero_idx = np.where((h==0) & (A==-1))[0]
       one_idx0 = np.where((h==1) & (B==0))[0]
       # generate pairs (A=-1,B=0)
       add_pairs(zero_idx, one_idx0, c_avoid=ell, target 15)
       zero_idx0 = ... A==0; one_idxm1=B==-1
       add...
       # maybe A=-1,B=-1 raw -2
       # if raw -1, avoid diagonal: add_pairs checks
    # random by probabilities from top lags?
    for _ in range(limit-len):
       i = random zero; j=random one; add
    return unique list
```

Local search update of C and h:
```
def apply_swap(h,C,i,j,off,n): # returns newC
    newC = C.copy()
    # use old h? We modify h after? Ensure delta uses old h. So update h after newC.
    # slices as above.
    newC[off:off+n-i] += 1 - h[i:]
    newC[off:off+i+1] -= h[i::-1]
    newC[off:off+n-j] -= 1 - h[j:]
    newC[off:off+j+1] += h[j::-1]
    newC[off] -= 2
    newC[off + j - i] += 1
    newC[off + i - j] += 1
    h[i], h[j] = 1,0? Wait candidate i zero ->1, j one->0. So h[i]=1;h[j]=0.
    return newC, h
```

Local search loop improvements:
```
def local_search_binary(h, C, M, score, deadline, max_rounds, seed):
   rng=RandomState(seed)
   h=h.copy(); C=C.copy(); M=int(M)
   best_h=h.copy(); best_C=C.copy(); best_M=M
   stagnant=0
   while time.time()<deadline and stagnant<max_stagnant:
       # generate candidates
       candidates = generate_candidates(h,C,M,n,off,rng,limit=2000)
       improved=False
       countM = np.count_nonzero(C>=M)
       for (i,j) in candidates:
          if h[i]!=0 or h[j]!=1: continue # generated based current but maybe duplicate after? no apply break
          newC = eval swap (without updating h maybe)
          newM = int(newC.max())
          if newM < M:
             h,C,M = apply; improved=True; break
          elif newM == M:
             cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(newC >= M))
             if cnt < countM:
                h,C,M... improved=True; break
       if improved:
          stagnant=0
          if M<best_M: update best
       else:
          # try random swaps maybe 100
          improved_rand=False
          countM = ...
          for r in range(random_trials):
             i=zero_rand; j=one_rand
             eval; if newM <= M and (newM<M or count_m decrease) accept; break
             # annealing accept newM==M+1 with prob if stagnant high?
          if improved_rand: stagnant small
          else:
             stagnant +=1
             # perturb: do k random swaps that don't increase M too much? Or restart from best with random perturb.
             # If stagnant > threshold, break to outer restart
             if stagnant > ...: break
   return best
```

`make_binary_from_continuous`:
```
def make_binary_from_continuous(cont, n):
    m=len(cont)
    if m==n: x_old = np.linspace(0,2,m); h interp? if same size, use cont
    else:
       x_old = np.linspace(0,2,m)
       x_new = np.linspace(0,2,n)
       vals = np.interp(x_new, x_old, cont)
    order = np.argsort(vals)
    h=np.zeros(n,int); h[order[n//2:]] =1
    return h
```
Using `np.interp` linear may create many near ties; threshold arbitrary but fine. For parent step function, linear interpolation slopes; threshold may produce extended blocks. Better use nearest for repeated: if n multiple, `h0_interp = np.interp` okay.

def random_run_binary(n, rng, avg_run=3):
   h=np.zeros(n,int)
   pos=0; val=1 if rng.random()<0.5 else 0
   while pos<n:
      L=rng.randint(max(1,avg_run-1), avg_run+2) # e.g. 2-4
      h[pos:min(n,pos+L)]=val
      val=1-val
      pos+=L
   # fix exact half: if not, flip random values? But runs may not exact. Need ensure sum n/2.

```
rng=RandomState(seed)
h=h.copy(); C=C.copy(); M=int(M)
best_h=h.copy(); best_M=M
no_prog=0
while time.time()<deadline:
    if M < best_M: best_h=h.copy(); best_M=M; no_prog=0
    old_score = int(np.sum(C[C >= M-2]))
    candidates = generate_candidates(..., limit=max_batch, rng)
    improved=False
    countM = int(np.count_nonzero(C >= M)) # maybe unused
    for idx,(i,j) in enumerate(candidates):
        if h[i]!=0 or h[j]!=1: continue
        newC = eval_swap(C,h,i,j,off,n)
        newM = int(newC.max())
        if newM < M:
            apply; improved=True; break
        elif newM == M:
            new_score = int(np.sum(newC[newC >= M-2]))
            if new_score < old_score:
                apply; improved=True; break
    if improved:
        no_prog=0
    else:
        no_prog +=1
        # random moves for escape
        zeros=np.where(h==0)[0]; ones=np.where(h==1)[0]
        deg=0
        for r in range(200):
            i=zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))]; j=ones[rng.randint(len(ones))]
            newC=eval...
            newM=int(newC.max())
            if newM < M:
               apply; improved=True; break
            elif newM == M:
                new_score=...
                if new_score < old_score:
                    apply; improved=True; break
            elif newM == M+1 and rng.random() < 0.05 * min(1, no_prog/20):
                apply accept increase; no_prog=0; break
        if improved: continue
        # perturbation if no_prog high
        if no_prog % 20 ==0:
            # perturb current by 10 random swaps, but only keep if M doesn't exceed best_M+3
            for _ in range(10):
                i,j; newC=eval
                newM=int(newC.max())
                if newM <= best_M + 3:
                    apply
            # after perturbation, if M>best_M maybe reset to best? Hmm.
        if no_prog > max_no_progress:
            # if perturbed didn't help, break this local search
            break
return best_h, best_M
```

Pseudo code:
```
def choose_high_lags(C, M, n, off, rng, max_lags=50):
    high = np.where(C >= M)[0]
    if len(high)==0: high = np.argsort(C)[-max_lags:]
    # include some M-1 if high too few
    if len(high) < max_lags:
       m1 = np.where((C == M-1))[0]
       # sample from m1
       if len(m1)>0:
         m1 = np.array(m1)
         take = min(len(m1), max_lags-len(high))
         idx = rng.choice(m1, take, replace=False)
         high = np.concatenate([high, idx])
    if len(high)>max_lags: high = high[:max_lags] # random order already? Could sample if more
    return high
```
Use C==M mostly. If many >max_lags, sample random to diversify.

```
def add_pair_set(cand, seen, zero_list, one_list, ell, count, rng, avoid):
    if len(zero_list)==0 or len(one_list)==0: return
    for _ in range(count*3):
       if len(add)>=count: break
       i=int(zero_list[rng.randint(len(zero_list))]); j=int(one_list[rng.randint(len(one_list))])
       if avoid and ((j-i)==ell or (j-i)==-ell): continue
       key=i*n+j; if key not seen: add
```
Need key int large but okay. Could use tuple; set of int faster.

Candidate generation:
```
def generate_candidates(h, C, M, n, off, rng, limit=800):
    cand=[]; seen=set()
    high=choose_high_lags(... max_lags=30)
    zmask=(h==0); omask=(h==1)
    for idx in high:
       ell=int(idx-off)
       pos=np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int8); neg=np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int8)
       if ell>=0:
          r=n-ell
          pos[:r] = 1 - h[ell:]
          neg[ell:] = h[:r]
       else:
          d=-ell; r=n-d
          pos[d:] = 1 - h[:r]
          neg[:r] = h[d:]
       A=pos-neg; B=-pos+neg
       zA_m1 = np.nonzero(zmask & (A==-1))[0]
       z0 = np.nonzero(zmask & (A==0))[0]
       oB_0 = np.nonzero(omask & (B==0))[0]
       oB_m1 = np.nonzero(omask & (B==-1))[0]
       add_pair(cand,seen,zA_m1,oB_m1,ell,4,rng,False)
       add_pair(cand,seen,zA_m1,oB_0,ell,4,rng,True)
       add_pair(cand,seen,z0,oB_m1,ell,4,rng,True)
       # if no candidates, maybe raw 0 with c? no
    # random candidates
    zeros=np.nonzero(zmask)[0]; ones=np.nonzero(omask)[0]
    for _ in range(min(200, limit-len(cand))):
       i=zeros[...] ; j=ones[...]
       add if unseen
    return cand
```
We can restrict `limit` to maybe 1000. But if high lags 30*12=360 +200 random=560. Fine.

Candidate eval function:
```
def eval_swap_max(C, h, i, j, off, n):
    newC = C.copy()
    omh = 1 - h
    newC[off-i:off+n-i] += omh
    newC[off+i-n+1:off+i+1] -= h[::-1]
    newC[off-j:off+n-j] -= omh
    newC[off+j-n+1:off+j+1] += h[::-1]
    newC[off] -= 2
    newC[off+j-i] += 1
    newC[off+i-j] += 1
    return int(newC.max()), newC
```
But creating `omh` each call length n; can pass `omh` computed once for h. In local loop, after accept, h changes, recompute. For candidate batch, pass same omh. `h_rev = h[::-1]` view can pass. However after h changed, h_rev invalid? If h updated in place, view reflects new; but we break after apply. For a batch, h unchanged during eval. Pass `omh` and `hrev`. Good.
```
def apply_swap_data(h, omh, hrev, C, i, j, off, n):
   newC = C.copy()
   newC[...] += omh
   newC[...] -= hrev
   newC[...] -= omh
   newC[...] += hrev
   ...
   h[i]=1; h[j]=0
   omh = 1 - h  # new
   hrev = h[::-1]
   return newC, omh, hrev
```
`hrev` view of h; after h[i],h[j] update, view updated. No need new.

import numpy as np
import time

def compute_c5_float(h, n):
    dx=2.0/n
    c=np.correlate(h.astype(float), 1.0-h, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(c)*dx)

def to_binary(h, n, seed=None):
    if h.size != n:
       old=np.linspace(0,2,h.size)
       new=np.linspace(0,2,n)
       v=np.interp(new, old, h)
    else: v=h.astype(float)
    if seed is not None and seed<0:
       rng=np.random.RandomState(seed); v=v+1e-6*rng.randn(n) # but top-level no closure, okay
    order=np.argsort(v, kind='stable')
    b=np.zeros(n,dtype=np.int32); b[order[n//2:]]=1
    # if sum not n//2 due ties? exactly
    # ensure if duplicate and n odd? even
    return b

def compute_counts(h,n):

   # int32

def randomize_h(h, n, k, rng):
    h=h.copy()
    for _ in range(k):
       zidx=np.where(h==0)[0]; oidx=np.where(h==1)[0]
       if len(zidx)==0 or len(oidx)==0: break
       i=zidx[rng.randint(len(zidx))]; j=oidx[rng.randint(len(oidx))]
       h[i],h[j]=1,0
    return h
```

Pseudo final:
```
import numpy as np
import time

def _empty(): ...

def compute_c5_general(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(c) * dx)

def cont_to_binary(vals, n, seed=None):
    vals = np.asarray(vals, dtype=float)
    m = vals.size
    if m == n:
        v = vals.copy()
    else:
        x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, m)
        x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n)
        v = np.interp(x_new, x_old, vals)
    if seed is not None:
        rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
        # add very small noise to break ties
        v = v + 1e-9 * rng.rand(n)
    order = np.argsort(v, kind='stable')
    b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int32)
    b[order[n//2:]] = 1
    # In unlikely case sum not n//2 (if n odd), adjust
    s = int(b.sum())
    target = n//2
    if s > target:
       idx = np.where(b==1)[0]
       for k in range(s-target): b[idx[k]]=0
    elif s < target:
       idx = np.where(b==0)[0]
       for k in range(target-s): b[idx[k]]=1
    return b

def compute_counts(h, n):
    a = h.astype(np.int32, copy=False)
    b = 1 - a
    c = np.correlate(a, b, mode='full')
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.int32)
    return c

def swap_newC(C, h, omh, i, j, off, n):
    newC = C.copy()
    newC[off - i : off + n - i] += omh
    newC[off + i - n + 1 : off + i + 1] -= h[::-1]
    newC[off - j : off + n - j] -= omh
    newC[off + j - n + 1 : off + j + 1] += h[::-1]
    newC[off] -= 2
    newC[off + j - i] += 1
    newC[off + i - j] += 1
    return newC, int(newC.max())
```
Check slice ranges and `omh` length n. For p first, start off-p may be negative? p max n-1 -> off-(n-1)=0; min p0->off. Good. stop off+n-p max off+n=2n-1; min p=n-1 -> off+1=n? L=2n-1 length, stop n okay. Wait for p=n-1, first valid b=0..n-1, idx off-p..off-p+n-1 = 0..n-2? But p=n-1, b=0 lag=-(n-1) idx0; b=n-1 lag=0 idx off=n-1. End exclusive off+n-p = (n-1)+n-(n-1)=n. So slice 0:n includes idx0..n-1. That's length n, but last idx n-1 corresponds b? idx n-1 -> lag0, b=p=n-1. Yes correct. So stop n not n? But off=n-1, stop=n, includes up to n-1. Good. For p=0, slice off..off+n = n-1..2n-1, includes idx n-1 (b=0 lag0) up to 2n-1 (b=n-1 lag n-1)? Wait p=0, b=0 lag0 idx off=n-1; b=n-1 lag n-1 idx off+n-1=2n-2. Stop off+n=2n-1 exclusive includes idx 2n-2. Good.
Second p: start off+p-n+1, stop off+p+1. For p=n-1, start off+n-1-n+1=off=n-1, stop off+n=2n-1, length n: idx n-1..2n-2 correspond a? p=n-1, lag p-a. a=0 lag n-1 idx 2n-2; a=n-1 lag0 idx n-1. Ascending slice idx n-1 (a=n-1) to 2n-2 (a=0); values h[::-1] = h[n-1..0] correct. For p=0, start0 stop1 idx0, value h[::-1] first element h[n-1]. p=0, second terms a? lag=-a idx -a? idx off-a. a=n-1 lag=-(n-1) idx0, value h[n-1]. correct.

`generate_candidates(h, C, M, off, n, rng, limit)`:
```
def generate_candidates(...):
    cand=[]; seen=set()
    # choose lags
    idxM = np.where(C == M)[0]
    idxM1 = np.where(C == M-1)[0]
    max_lags = min(80, max(30, n//4))
    if len(idxM) > max_lags:
       idxM = idxM[rng.randint(len(idxM), max_lags)] ? rng.choice better.
    high=[]
    if len(idxM)>0: high.extend(idxM.tolist())
    remaining = max_lags - len(high)
    if remaining >0 and len(idxM1)>0:
       take = min(remaining, len(idxM1))
       if take < len(idxM1): idxM1 = idxM1[rng.randint...]
       high.extend(idxM1[:take].tolist())
    if len(high)>max_lags: high=high[:max_lags]
    # order high maybe descending? all same M/M-1. Could shuffle.
    if len(high)>1:
       order = rng.permutation(len(high)); high = [high[k] for k in order]
    zmask = (h == 0); omask = (h == 1)
    for idx in high:
       ell = int(idx - off)
       pos = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int8); neg = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int8)
       if ell >= 0:
           r = n - ell
           pos[:r] = 1 - h[ell:]
           neg[ell:] = h[:r]
       else:
           d = -ell; r = n - d
           pos[d:] = 1 - h[:r]
           neg[:r] = h[d:]
       A = pos - neg
       B = -pos + neg
       zA_m1 = np.nonzero(zmask & (A == -1))[0]
       z0 = np.nonzero(zmask & (A == 0))[0]
       oB_0 = np.nonzero(omask & (B == 0))[0]
       oB_m1 = np.nonzero(omask & (B == -1))[0]
       add_pair_candidates(cand, seen, zA_m1, oB_m1, ell, 3, n, rng, avoid=False)
       add_pair_candidates(cand, seen, zA_m1, oB_0, ell, 3, n, rng, avoid=True)
       add_pair_candidates(cand, seen, z0, oB_m1, ell, 3, n, rng, avoid=True)
       if len(cand) >= limit: break
    # random
    zeros = np.nonzero(zmask)[0]; ones = np.nonzero(omask)[0]
    rcount = min(max(100, limit//2), n//4)
    for _ in range(rcount):
        if len(cand)>=limit: break
        i = int(zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))]); j=int(ones[rng.randint(len(ones))])
        key=i*n+j
        if key not in seen:
           seen.add(key); cand.append((i,j))
    return cand
```
Need if idxM empty? C max M so not.

`local_search_binary` implementation details:
```
def local_search_binary(h, C, M, deadline, seed, limit_factor=4):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    n = h.size
    off = n - 1
    h = h.copy(); C=C.astype(np.int32, copy=False)
    M = int(C.max())
    omh = 1 - h
    best_h = h.copy(); best_M = M
    no_prog = 0
    max_no_prog = 80
    limit = int(min(2000, max(300, n * limit_factor)))
    while time.time() < deadline:
        if M <= best_M: # if equal no_prog? best_M maybe lower
            if M < best_M or no_prog>...:
        # update best
        if M < best_M:
            best_h = h.copy(); best_M = M; no_prog = 0
        high_threshold = max(0, M-2)
        old_score = int(np.sum(C[C >= high_threshold]))
        countM = int(np.count_nonzero(C >= M))
        candidates = generate_candidates(h,C,M,off,n,rng,limit)
        if not candidates:
            # random maybe
        improved=False
        for (i,j) in candidates:
            if time.time() > deadline: break
            if h[i] != 0 or h[j] != 1: continue
            newC, newM = swap_newC(C,h,omh,i,j,off,n)
            if newM < M:
                accept=True
            elif newM == M:
                new_score = int(np.sum(newC[newC >= high_threshold]))
                # also ensure not increasing max? newM==M
                if new_score < old_score:
                    accept=True
                else:
                    accept=False
            else:
                accept=False
            if accept:
                C=newC; M=newM; h[i]=1; h[j]=0; omh=1-h; improved=True
                break
        if improved:
            no_prog=0
            if M < best_M: best_h=h.copy(); best_M=M
            continue
        # no improved from candidates
        no_prog += 1
        # random trial with possible anneal
        zeros = np.nonzero(h==0)[0]; ones=np.nonzero(h==1)[0]
        trials = 100
        for t in range(trials):
            if time.time() > deadline: break
            i=int(zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))]); j=int(ones[rng.randint(len(ones))])
            newC, newM = swap_newC(C,h,omh,i,j,off,n)
            if newM < M:
                accept=True
            elif newM == M:
                new_score = int(np.sum(newC[newC >= high_threshold]))
                accept = new_score < old_score
            else:
                # anneal accept if small increase and stale
                if M + (1 if no_prog>10 else 2) >= newM and no_prog > 10 and rng.random() < min(0.15, 0.02*(no_prog-10)):
                    accept=True
                else: accept=False
            if accept:
                C=newC; M=newM; h[i]=1; h[j]=0; omh=1-h; improved=True; no_prog=0
                break
        if improved:
            if M > best_M:
                # if annealed above best, reset no_prog? continue but maybe later
                pass
            else:
                best_h=h.copy(); best_M=M
            continue
        # no improvement or anneal accepted? if improved via anneal newM higher, best unchanged but current.
        # Wait improved True set if accept came from newM<M or newM==M score or anneal. If anneal newM > M, best unchanged but improved True. We'll continue.
        if not improved:
            # perturb if stale
            if no_prog > 20 and no_prog % 20 == 0:
                # try up to 10 random swaps that keep M <= best_M+4
                for _ in range(10):
                    if time.time()>deadline: break
                    # recompute z/o
                    z = np.nonzero(h==0)[0]; o=np.nonzero(h==1)[0]
                    i=...; j=...
                    newC,newM=swap_newC(...)
                    if newM <= best_M + 4:
                        C=newC; M=newM; h[i]=1; h[j]=0
                        # restart no_prog? 
                omh=1-h
                no_prog=0
            if no_prog > max_no_prog:
                break
    return best_h, best_M
```

def optimize_binary(n, vals, deadline, seed, target_c):
    tstart=time.time()
    h=cont_to_binary(vals,n,seed)
    C=compute_counts(h,n); M=int(C.max())
    best_h=h.copy(); best_M=M
    omh? not needed
    # attempt loop
    attempt=0
    while time.time() < deadline - 3:
        if M < best_M: ... (local_search returns best)
        # choose inner deadline: leave some for attempts
        # if attempt==0, allow deadline - reserve (e.g. min(remaining*0.7, ...))
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        if attempt == 0:
            inner_dl = deadline - max(30, int(0.25*rem))? This complicates.
        # simpler: run local_search with deadline, but it may consume all. For restarts, after it returns due break, use remaining.
        h2,M2=local_search_binary(h,C,M,deadline,seed+attempt)
        if M2 < best_M: best_h=h2.copy(); best_M=M2
        # if best_M reaches a very low or time low, break
        if time.time() > deadline - 20: break
        # randomize best for next
        k = 8 + int(rng.integers(0, 25))
        h = randomize_binary(best_h, n, k, seed+100+attempt)
        C=compute_counts(h,n); M=int(C.max())
        # If randomize M too high > best_M+15, maybe reduce k? But okay.
        attempt +=1
        if attempt >= 4: break
    return best_h, best_M

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    deadline = t0 + max(1.0, budget_s - 5)
    best_h=None; best_c=np.inf; best_n=0
    try:
       parent=np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
    except Exception:
       parent=None
    if parent is None:
       # create random binary at n=310 as continuous
       n_default=310
       parent=random_initial_binary(n_default, seed).astype(float)
    # Choose n choices based on current best?

       c = 2*M/float(n)

def blend_starts(parent, best_h, best_n, n):

Functions:
```
def compute_c5_general(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if h.size != n: n=h.size
    dx = 2.0/n
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(c) * dx)
```

```
def interp_values(vals, n):
    vals = np.asarray(vals, dtype=float)
    m = vals.size
    if m == 0: return np.zeros(n)
    if m == n: return vals.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, m)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n)
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, vals)
```

`choose_high_lags(C, M, n, rng, max_lags)`:
```
def choose_high_lags(...):
    high=[]
    idxM = np.where(C == M)[0]
    if len(idxM) > max_lags:
       idxM = idxM[rng.choice(len(idxM), max_lags, replace=False)]
    high.extend(idxM.tolist())
    rem = max_lags - len(high)
    if rem > 0:
       idxM1 = np.where((C == M-1) & (M-1 >=0))[0]
       if len(idxM1)>0:
          take = min(rem, len(idxM1))
          if take < len(idxM1): idxM1=idxM1[rng.choice(len(idxM1), take, replace=False)]
          high.extend(idxM1[:take].tolist())
    if len(high) > 1:
       perm = rng.permutation(len(high))
       high = [high[int(k)] for k in perm]
    return high
```

   i = int(z[rng.randint(len(z))]); j = int(o[rng.randint(len(o))])

return h

MILP function:
```
def milp_binary(n, limit_s, seed):
    if cp is None or n % 2 !=0: return None, None, None
    t0=time.time()
    try:
       hvar=cp.Variable(n, boolean=True)
       total=0; offsets=[]
       for d in range(1,n):
          offsets.append(total); total += n-d
       yvar=cp.Variable(total, nonneg=True, upper=1)
       Mvar=cp.Variable(integer=True, nonneg=True)
       cons=[cp.sum(hvar) == n/2]
       for d in range(1,n):
          L=n-d
          s=offsets[d-1]; yd=yvar[s:s+L]
          left=hvar[0:L]
          right=hvar[d:d+L]
          cons.append(yd <= left)
          cons.append(yd <= right)
          cons.append(yd >= left + right - 1)
          cons.append(cp.sum(left) - cp.sum(yd) <= Mvar)
          cons.append(cp.sum(right) - cp.sum(yd) <= Mvar)
       prob=cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(Mvar), cons)
       solvers = []
       for name in ['CBC','SCIP','GUROBI','MOSEK','Xpress','XPRESS']:
          s = getattr(cp, name, None)
          if s is not None: solvers.append(s)
       last_err=None
       for s in solvers:
          if time.time() - t0 > limit_s*0.8: break
          try:
             prob.solve(solver=s, verbose=False, solver_options={'time_limit': max(10, int(limit_s - (time.time()-t0)))})
             if hvar.value is not None:
                hv = np.rint(np.asarray(hvar.value)).astype(int)
                # fix sum
                # compute C
                C=compute_counts(hv,n); mM=int(C.max())
                return hv,mM,C
          except Exception as e: last_err=e
       return None,None,None
    except Exception:
       return None,None,None
```

`Mvar integer` with nonneg; objective integer; solver may not support pure? CBC yes.

def fix_binary_sum(h, n):

  if s>target:
     one_idx=np.where(h==1)[0]
     # flip maybe random? deterministic from end? Use positions that affect? choose last s-target
     for k in range(a,i): h[one_idx[k]]=0
  elif s<target: h[zero_idx[:target-s]]=1
  return h
```

```
def optimize_n(n, start_vals, deadline, seed):
    rng = RandomState(seed)
    h = values_to_binary(start_vals, n, rng, jitter=1e-8)
    C=compute_counts; M=...
    best_h=h.copy(); best_M=M
    attempt=0
    while time<deadline:
        h_cur,C_cur,M_cur = local_search_binary(h, C, M, deadline, seed+attempt)
        if M_cur < best_M: best...
        if time>deadline-15: break
        # if M_cur == best_M and probably stuck, perturb best
        k = 5 + rng.randint(0,20)
        h = randomize_binary(best_h, n, k, rng)
        C = compute_counts(h,n); M=int(C.max())
        # If randomized M too high > best_M+8, maybe reduce k or use less? We can choose a smaller perturbation if M high; loop attempts.
        if M > best_M + 10:
            k = max(1, k//2); h=randomize...; compute
        attempt +=1
        if attempt >= 6: break
    return best_h,best_M
```

Let's define `high_score(C, M, band=2)` top-level:
```
def high_score(C, M, band=2):
    thr = M - band
    if thr < 0: thr = 0
    return int(np.sum(C[C >= thr]))
```

`random_run_binary`:
```
def random_run_binary(n, seed, avg=3):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    h=np.zeros(n,dtype=np.int32)
    pos=0; val=rng.randint(0,2)
    while pos<n:
       low=max(1, avg-2); high=avg+2
       L=rng.randint(low, high+1)
       h[pos:min(n,pos+L)] = val
       val = 1-val; pos += L
    # fix sum
    target=n//2; s=h.sum()
    if s>target:
       idx=np.where(h==1)[0]
       # flip some ones; choose random to preserve? deterministic rng
       perm=rng.permutation(len(idx)); for p in perm[:s-target]: h[idx[p]]=0
    elif s<target:
       idx=np.where(h==0)[0]; perm=...; set ones
    return h
```
Random run lengths avg3; can start/end diff; fix sum may create short runs. Good.

Let's implement `values_to_binary`:
```
def values_to_binary(vals, n, rng=None, jitter=0.0):
    vals = np.asarray(vals, dtype=float)
    m = vals.size
    if m != n:
        if m == 0:
            vals = np.zeros(n)
        else:
            x_old = np.linspace(0,2,m)
            x_new = np.linspace(0,2,n)
            vals = np.interp(x_new, x_old, vals)
    if jitter > 0 and rng is not None:
        vals = vals + jitter * rng.rand(n)
    order = np.argsort(vals, kind='stable')
    target = n // 2
    b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int32)
    b[order[target:]] = 1
    s = int(b.sum())
    if s != target:
        # adjust
        if s > target:
            idx = np.where(b == 1)[0]
            # flip last s-target in idx (deterministic)
            for k in range(s-target): b[idx[k]]=0
        else:
            idx = np.where(b == 0)[0]
            for k in range(target-s): b[idx[k]]=1
    return b
```
If n odd? We choose even. But if not, target floor; sum not exact n/2. We'll only use even. Fine.

`compute_c5_general` for int h: `np.correlate(h.astype(float), 1.0-h, 'full')`. For n, ensure h size.

`compute_counts` if h int32; if h float binary? Cast.
```
def compute_counts(h, n=None):
   h = np.asarray(h)
   if n is None: n=h.size
   a = h.astype(np.int32, copy=False)
   # if values not exactly 0/1 but fractional, product not counts; use for restart? Only binary.
   if np.any((a<0)|(a>1)) or np.any((a*?))? If float with 0.5, astype truncates! So use only for binary int. For randomize best binary. Good.
   b = 1 - a
   c = np.correlate(a,b,mode='full')
   return np.asarray(c, dtype=np.int32)
```
If randomize on binary only. Parent not used for compute_counts.

`local_search_binary` details with `high_score` best update:
```
def local_search_binary(h, C, M, deadline, seed, limit=None):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    n = h.size; off=n-1
    h = h.astype(np.int32, copy=True)
    C = C.astype(np.int32, copy=True)
    M = int(C.max())
    if limit is None: limit = int(min(900, max(300, 3*n)))
    best_h = h.copy(); best_M = M; best_C = C.copy()
    best_score = high_score(C, M)
    no_prog = 0
    max_no_prog = 150
    while time.time() < deadline:
        cur_score = high_score(C, M)
        if M < best_M or (M == best_M and cur_score < best_score):
             best_M=M; best_score=cur_score; best_h=h.copy(); best_C=C.copy()
        old_score = cur_score
        old_countM = int(np.count_nonzero(C >= M))
        omh = 1 - h
        candidates = generate_candidates(h,C,M,off,n,rng,limit)
        improved = False
        for i,j in candidates:
            if time.time() > deadline: break
            if h[i] != 0 or h[j] != 1: continue
            newC, newM = swap_newC(C,h,omh,i,j,off,n)
            accept = False
            if newM < M:
                accept = True
            elif newM == M:
                new_score = high_score(newC, M)
                if new_score < old_score:
                    accept = True
                else:
                    new_countM = int(np.count_nonzero(newC >= M))
                    if new_countM < old_countM:
                        accept = True
            if accept:
                C = newC; M = newM; h[i]=1; h[j]=0; improved=True; no_prog=0; break
        if improved:
            # if M decreased or score improved, continue. best updated next loop.
            continue
        no_prog += 1
        # random/anneal
        zeros = np.nonzero(h==0)[0]; ones = np.nonzero(h==1)[0]
        if zeros.size and ones.size:
            ntrials = 80 + min(200, no_prog*5)
            for t in range(ntrials):
                if time.time() > deadline: break
                i = int(zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))]); j=int(ones[rng.randint(len(ones))])
                newC, newM = swap_newC(C,h,omh,i,j,off,n)
                accept=False
                if newM < M: accept=True
                elif newM == M:
                    new_score = high_score(newC,M)
                    if new_score < old_score: accept=True
                else:
                    if no_prog > 20 and newM <= M + (1 if no_prog < 60 else 2):
                        p = min(0.15, 0.01 * (no_prog - 20))
                        if rng.random() < p:
                            accept=True
                if accept:
                    C=newC; M=newM; h[i]=1; h[j]=0; improved=True; no_prog=0
                    break
        if improved:
            continue
        # stale
        if no_prog > 20 and no_prog % 20 == 0:
            # perturb current
            maxM = best_M + 5
            for _ in range(8):
                if time.time() > deadline: break
                z=np.nonzero(h==0)[0]; o=np.nonzero(h==1)[0]
                if not z.size or not o.size: break
                i=int(z[rng.randint(len(z))]); j=int(o[rng.randint(len(o))])
                newC,newM=swap_newC(C,h,1-h,i,j,off,n) # use current omh? if apply multiple, h changes; recompute each.
                if newM <= maxM:
                    C=newC; M=newM; h[i]=1; h[j]=0
            no_prog=0
            if M > best_M + 8:
                h = best_h.copy(); C = best_C.copy(); M = best_M
        if no_prog > max_no_prog:
            break
    # final check best maybe current better? top update not after last if loop ended. do update
    cur_score=high_score(C,M)
    if M < best_M or (M==best_M and cur_score < best_score): ...
    return best_h, best_M, best_C
```
In perturbation loop, using `1-h` each swap; if multiple accepted, `omh` not updated but we pass `1-h` current h (not updated? We update h after each accept, and in next iteration `1-h` current). Good. But if we use `newM <= maxM` and M changes, maxM constant best_M+5. If M increases above, next swaps maybe still allowed? We break only if no accept? It could wander. Good.
If after perturbation M > best_M+8, reset to best. But what about C? best_C consistent. Good.

Implement quick scan:
```
def quick_threshold_scan(parent, deadline, seed):
    best_c=np.inf; best_h=None; best_n=0; c_by_n={}
    tstart=time.time()
    max_n=400
    for n in range(120, max_n+1, 2):
       if time.time() > deadline - 60: break
       h = values_to_binary(parent, n, None, 0.0)
       M = int(compute_counts(h,n).max())
       c = 2.0*M/float(n)
       if n not in c_by_n or c < c_by_n[n][0]: c_by_n[n]=(c,h.copy()) # storing h for all n memory: 400*400 ints ~640KB ok.
       if c < best_c: best_c=c; best_h=h.copy(); best_n=n
    # top jitter
    top = sorted(c_by_n.items(), key=lambda x:x[1][0])[:6]
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    for n,(c0,h0) in top:
       if time.time()>deadline-20: break
       for tr in range(5):
          h=values_to_binary(parent,n,rng,jitter=1e-7+tr*1e-6)
          M=int(compute_counts(h,n).max()); c=2*M/n
          if c < c_by_n[n][0]: c_by_n[n]=(c,h.copy())
          if c < best_c: best_c=c; best_h=h.copy(); best_n=n
    return best_h,best_c,best_n,c_by_n
```
Storing h in c_by_n for all n maybe okay but h arrays. 141 arrays avg 260 length ~36k ints <0.5MB. Good.

def run(...):
  t0=... deadline=t0+budget_s-4
  try parent=...
  except: parent=None
  if parent is None: parent=random_run_binary(142,seed).astype(float)
  # quick scan
  qh, qc, qn, c_by_n = quick_threshold_scan(parent, deadline, seed)
  if qh is not None and qc < inf:
      best_h=qh.copy(); best_c=qc; best_n=qn
  else:
      best_h=parent.copy(); best_c=compute_c5_general(best_h,parent.size); best_n=parent.size
  # Also consider original parent (nonbinary) if better (maybe continuous better than threshold? possible)
  if parent is not None:
      cp=compute_c5_general(parent,parent.size)
      if cp < best_c: best_h=parent.copy(); best_c=cp; best_n=parent.size
  # Build phase ns
  phase_ns=[]
  if best_n%2==0 and 80<=best_n<=500: phase_ns.append(best_n)
  for n in [142,158,200,310,134,128]:
     if n not in phase_ns: phase_ns.append(n)
  # remove >400? keep if best_n maybe 400. Could cap 500.
  phase_ns=[n for n in phase_ns if n%2==0 and 80<=n<=500]
  if not phase_ns: phase_ns=[142]
  # fractions: distribute among up to 4? If many, use smaller.
  # We want at least 4 phases. Use first 5.
  phase_ns=phase_ns[:5]
  # fractions based on index and total: e.g. [0.5,0.25,0.1,0.075,0.075]
  fracs=[0.5,0.25,0.12,0.075,0.055][:len(phase_ns)]
  for n,frac in zip(phase_ns,fracs):
     rem=deadline-time.time()
     if rem<25: break
     phase_dl = time.time() + max(12.0, frac*rem)
     if phase_dl > deadline: phase_dl=deadline
     h_new,M_new = optimize_n(n, parent, best_h, best_n, phase_dl, seed*17+n)
     if h_new is not None:
        c_new=compute_c5_general(h_new,n)
        if c_new < best_c - 1e-12:
           best_c=c_new; best_h=h_new.copy(); best_n=n
  # final polish if time
  if deadline - time.time() > 40:
     n_fin = best_n if (best_n%2==0 and 80<=best_n<=500) else 142
     h_new,M_new=optimize_n(n_fin,parent,best_h,best_n,deadline-3,seed+424242)
     if h_new is not None:
        c_new=compute_c5_general(h_new,n_fin)
        if c_new < best_c: update
  # final ensure c
  if best_h is None: ...
  # If best_h is binary int, compute c exact with float; if best_h parent float, c computed.
  return best_h, float(best_c), int(best_n)
```

def quick_threshold_scan(parent, deadline, seed):
    c_by_n = {}
    best_c=inf; best_h=None; best_n=0
    for n in range(120, 401, 2):
       if time.time() > deadline - 30: break
       h=values_to_binary(parent,n,None,0.0)
       M=int(compute_counts(h,n).max()); c=2*M/float(n)
       if n not in c_by_n or c < c_by_n[n]: c_by_n[n]=c
       if c < best_c: best_c=c; best_h=h.copy(); best_n=n
    top = sorted(c_by_n.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1])[:6]
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed+7)
    for n,c0 in top:
       if time.time() > deadline - 10: break
       for tr in range(6):
          h=values_to_binary(parent,n,rng,jitter=1e-8*(tr+1))
          M=...; c=...
          if c < c_by_n.get(n,inf): c_by_n[n]=c
          if c < best_c: best_c=c; best_h=h.copy(); best_n=n
    return best_h,best_c,best_n,c_by_n
```

Detailed functions final:

```
def compute_c5_general(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if n is None or int(n) != h.size:
        n = h.size
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(c) * dx)
```

`interp_values`:
```
def interp_values(vals, n):
    vals=np.asarray(vals,dtype=float)
    m=vals.size
    if m==0:
       return np.zeros(n)
    if m==n:
       return vals.copy()
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,m)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n)
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, vals)
```

`values_to_binary` as above, using loops for adjust.
Need if n even? target = n//2; if n odd, sum not n/2. We can force sum target floor? The evaluation requires sum * dx =1 exactly => sum = n/2, so n must be even. We only use even. If called odd, floor not feasible. Could if n odd, use n-1? Not. Ensure run uses even. Quick range even. phase_ns even. If parent length odd? final parent maybe infeasible? Parent likely even. For fallback parent, if parent size odd and sum maybe? But initial likely valid. We can if n odd in final not use? If best_h parent odd, evaluate maybe sum not exact but pre-imported likely valid. We can ignore.

`compute_counts`:
```
def compute_counts(h, n=None):
    h=np.asarray(h)
    if n is None: n=h.size
    a=h.astype(np.int32, copy=False)
    # If any not 0/1? If float 0.5, astype truncates. But only binary.
    b=1-a
    return np.asarray(np.correlate(a,b,mode='full'), dtype=np.int32)
```

`high_score`:
```
def high_score(C, M, band=2):
    thr = M - band
    if thr < 0: thr = 0
    return int(np.sum(C[C >= thr]))
```

`add_candidate_pairs` top-level with n param:
```
def add_candidate_pairs(cand, seen, zlist, olist, ell, count, n, rng, avoid):
    lz=len(zlist); lo=len(olist)
    if lz==0 or lo==0: return
    added=0; max_attempts=count*12+5
    attempts=0
    while added < count and attempts < max_attempts:
       attempts +=1
       i=int(zlist[rng.randint(lz)])
       j=int(olist[rng.randint(lo)])
       if avoid and ((j-i)==ell or (j-i)==-ell):
           continue
       key=i*n+j
       if key not in seen:
           seen.add(key); cand.append((i,j)); added+=1
```

`choose_high_lags`:
```
def choose_high_lags(C, M, n, rng, max_lags):
    high=[]
    idxM=np.where(C == M)[0]
    if len(idxM) > max_lags:
       idxM=idxM[rng.choice(len(idxM), max_lags, replace=False)]
    for idx in idxM: high.append(int(idx))
    rem=max_lags-len(high)
    if rem>0 and M>1:
       idxM1=np.where(C == M-1)[0]
       idxM1=idxM1[idxM1 >=0]? C indices always positive. Need filter C value M-1 >0? C nonneg. Use `idxM1 = np.where((C == M-1))[0]`
       if len(idxM1)>0:
          take=min(rem,len(idxM1))
          if take < len(idxM1): idxM1=idxM1[rng.choice(len(idxM1), take, replace=False)]
          for idx in idxM1: high.append(int(idx))
    if len(high) > 1:
       perm=rng.permutation(len(high))
       high2=[]
       for k in perm: high2.append(high[int(k)])
       high=high2
    return high
```
If C==M-1 includes many zero counts if M=1; but M>1. For M=2, M-1=1, okay. If M=1, no. Good.

`generate_candidates` with high lags, limit. Use `dtypes`.
```
def generate_candidates(h, C, M, off, n, rng, limit):
    cand=[]; seen=set()
    max_lags = min(60, max(20, n//5))
    high = choose_high_lags(C,M,n,rng,max_lags)
    zmask=(h==0); omask=(h==1)
    per_case=3
    for idx in high:
       ell=int(idx-off)
       pos=np.zeros(n,dtype=np.int8); neg=np.zeros(n,dtype=np.int8)
       if ell >= 0:
          r=n-ell
          if r>0:
             pos[:r] = 1 - h[ell:]
             neg[ell:] = h[:r]
       else:
          d=-ell; r=n-d
          if r>0:
             pos[d:] = 1 - h[:r]
             neg[:r] = h[d:]
       A=pos-neg
       B=-pos+neg
       zA_m1=np.nonzero(zmask & (A == -1))[0]
       zA_0=np.nonzero(zmask & (A == 0))[0]
       oB_0=np.nonzero(omask & (B == 0))[0]
       oB_m1=np.nonzero(omask & (B == -1))[0]
       add_candidate_pairs(cand,seen,zA_m1,oB_m1,ell,per_case,n,rng,False)
       add_candidate_pairs(cand,seen,zA_m1,oB_0,ell,per_case,n,rng,True)
       add_candidate_pairs(cand,seen,zA_0,oB_m1,ell,per_case,n,rng,True)
       if len(cand) >= limit: break
    zeros=np.nonzero(zmask)[0]; ones=np.nonzero(omask)[0]
    if zeros.size and ones.size:
       rmin=min(limit-len(cand), max(60, n//4))
       attempts=0; max_attempts=5*rmin
       while len(cand)<limit and attempts<max_attempts:
          attempts+=1
          i=int(zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))]); j=int(ones[rng.randint(len(ones))])
          key=i*n+j
          if key not in seen:
             seen.add(key); cand.append((i,j))
    return cand
```
`A==0` may include (first=second=0/1), raw -1 possible. Good.

Could `B` have value -1? B=-pos+neg. If pos=1,neg=0 =>-1. Good.

`swap_newC` as above.

`local_search_binary` as described. Need ensure `omh` after perturbation reset to best? If reset h/C/M to best, continue loop; top recomputes omh. Good.

Let's write local_search code carefully; no syntax errors.

```
def local_search_binary(h, C, M, deadline, seed, limit=None):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    n = int(h.size); off = n-1
    h = h.astype(np.int32, copy=True)
    C = C.astype(np.int32, copy=True)
    M = int(C.max())
    if limit is None:
       if n <= 200:
          limit = int(min(900, max(300, 3*n)))
       else:
          limit = int(min(1200, max(500, 2*n)))
    max_no_prog = 180 if n <= 200 else 80
    best_h = h.copy(); best_C = C.copy(); best_M = M; best_score = high_score(C,M)
    no_prog = 0
    while time.time() < deadline:
       cur_score = high_score(C,M)
       if M < best_M or (M == best_M and cur_score < best_score):
           best_M=M; best_score=cur_score; best_h=h.copy(); best_C=C.copy()
       old_score=cur_score
       old_countM=int(np.count_nonzero(C >= M))
       omh = 1 - h
       candidates = generate_candidates(h, C, M, off, n, rng, limit)
       improved=False
       for pair in candidates:
          if time.time() > deadline: break
          i=pair[0]; j=pair[1]
          if h[i] != 0 or h[j] != 1: continue
          newC, newM = swap_newC(C, h, omh, i, j, off, n)
          acc=False
          if newM < M:
             acc=True
          elif newM == M:
             new_score = high_score(newC, M)
             if new_score < old_score:
                acc=True
             else:
                new_countM = int(np.count_nonzero(newC >= M))
                if new_countM < old_countM:
                   acc=True
          if acc:
             C=newC; M=newM; h[i]=1; h[j]=0; improved=True; no_prog=0
             break
       if improved:
          continue
       no_prog += 1
       zeros=np.nonzero(h==0)[0]; ones=np.nonzero(h==1)[0]
       if zeros.size and ones.size:
          ntrials = 80 + min(300, no_prog*5)
          for t in range(ntrials):
             if time.time() > deadline: break
             i=int(zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))]); j=int(ones[rng.randint(len(ones))])
             newC,newM=swap_newC(C,h,omh,i,j,off,n)
             acc=False
             if newM < M:
                acc=True
             elif newM == M:
                new_score=high_score(newC,M)
                if new_score < old_score: acc=True
                else:
                   new_countM=int(np.count_nonzero(newC >= M))
                   if new_countM < old_countM: acc=True
             else:
                if no_prog > 20 and newM <= M + (1 if no_prog < 60 else 2):
                    p=0.01*(no_prog-20)
                    if p > 0.15: p=0.15
                    if rng.random() < p: acc=True
             if acc:
                C=newC; M=newM; h[i]=1; h[j]=0; improved=True; no_prog=0
                break
       if improved:
          continue
       if no_prog > 20 and no_prog % 20 == 0:
          maxM = best_M + 5
          for _ in range(8):
             if time.time() > deadline: break
             z=np.nonzero(h==0)[0]; o=np.nonzero(h==1)[0]
             if z.size==0 or o.size==0: break
             i=int(z[rng.randint(len(z))]); j=int(o[rng.randint(len(o))])
             newC,newM=swap_newC(C,h,1-h,i,j,off,n)
             if newM <= maxM:
                C=newC; M=newM; h[i]=1; h[j]=0
          no_prog=0
          if M > best_M + 8:
             h=best_h.copy(); C=best_C.copy(); M=best_M
       if no_prog > max_no_prog:
          break
    cur_score=high_score(C,M)
    if M < best_M or (M == best_M and cur_score < best_score):
       best_M=M; best_score=cur_score; best_h=h.copy(); best_C=C.copy()
    return best_h, best_M, best_C
```
One issue: In perturbation loop, we use `swap_newC(C,h,1-h,...)` and then if accepted update h; next iteration uses updated h and C. But `1-h` computed before swap into expression? Python evaluates arguments left-to-right: `C,h,1-h,i,j,...` computes `1-h` before h update, okay. After update, next iteration recomputes. Good.
If multiple perturb swaps accepted, M changes; maxM constant. Good.

`random_run_binary`:
```
def random_run_binary(n, seed, avg=3):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    h=np.zeros(n,dtype=np.int32)
    pos=0; val=int(rng.randint(0,2))
    while pos < n:
       low=max(1, avg-2); high=avg+2
       L=int(rng.randint(low, high+1))
       end=pos+L
       if end>n: end=n
       h[pos:end]=val
       val=1-val
       pos=end
    target=n//2; s=int(h.sum())
    if s > target:
       idx=np.where(h==1)[0]
       perm=rng.permutation(len(idx))
       cnt=s-target
       for k in range(cnt): h[int(idx[perm[k]])]=0
    elif s < target:
       idx=np.where(h==0)[0]
       perm=rng.permutation(len(idx))
       cnt=target-s
       for k in range(cnt): h[int(idx[perm[k]])]=1
    # maybe if target=0? n small; ok
    return h
```
If n=1 target0, but even n only. Good.

`optimize_n`:
```
def optimize_n(n, parent_vals, prev_h, prev_n, deadline, seed):
    t_enter=time.time()
    if deadline - t_enter < 5 or n < 2:
        return None, None
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    v_parent=interp_values(parent_vals,n)
    starts=[]
    if prev_h is not None and len(prev_h)>0:
       if int(prev_n) == int(n):
          v_prev=np.asarray(prev_h,dtype=float).copy()
          starts.append(v_prev)
          mix=np.empty(n,dtype=float)
          for i in range(n): mix[i]=0.5*v_prev[i]+0.5*v_parent[i]
          starts.append(mix)
       else:
          v_prev=interp_values(prev_h,n)
          mix=np.empty(n,dtype=float)
          for i in range(n): mix[i]=0.5*v_parent[i]+0.5*v_prev[i]
          starts.append(mix)
          starts.append(v_parent)
    else:
       starts.append(v_parent)
    # add random run if room
    if n <= 600:
       starts.append(random_run_binary(n, seed+1234, 3).astype(float))
    # cap starts to 4
    if len(starts) > 4: starts=starts[:4]
    best_h=None; best_M=10**9; best_C=None; best_score=10**18
    initial_h=None
    max_rounds = len(starts) + 4
    total_budget = deadline - t_enter
    for round_idx in range(max_rounds):
       if time.time() > deadline - 8: break
       if round_idx < len(starts):
          v=starts[round_idx]
          h=values_to_binary(v,n,rng,jitter=1e-6)
       else:
          if best_h is None:
             if initial_h is None:
                h=values_to_binary(starts[0] if len(starts) else v_parent,n,rng,jitter=1e-6)
             else:
                h=initial_h.copy()
          else:
             k=5 + int(rng.randint(0,25))
             if round_idx > 6: k=max(3, k//2)
             h=randomize_binary(best_h,n,k,rng)
       C=compute_counts(h,n); M=int(C.max())
       if initial_h is None: initial_h=h.copy()
       sc=high_score(C,M)
       if M < best_M or (M == best_M and sc < best_score):
          best_M=M; best_score=sc; best_h=h.copy(); best_C=C.copy()
       # Allocate inner deadline based on remaining
       rem = deadline - time.time()
       if rem <= 5: break
       # fraction: 0.55 first, 0.25 second, 0.15 third, after random 0.1 each? Use simple:
       if round_idx == 0: frac=0.55
       elif round_idx == 1: frac=0.30
       elif round_idx == 2: frac=0.15
       else: frac=0.12
       inner_dl = time.time() + max(10.0, frac * rem)
       if inner_dl > deadline: inner_dl = deadline
       h2,M2,C2 = local_search_binary(h,C,M,inner_dl,seed*1000+round_idx)
       if h2 is not None:
          sc2=high_score(C2,M2)
          if M2 < best_M or (M2 == best_M and sc2 < best_score):
             best_M=M2; best_score=sc2; best_h=h2.copy(); best_C=C2.copy()
       if M2 < best_M? already.
       # If best_M very low and round_idx >= len(starts)? maybe continue for lower until deadline.
    if best_h is None:
       # fallback
       h=values_to_binary(starts[0] if len(starts) else v_parent,n,rng,jitter=0)
       C=compute_counts(h,n); M=int(C.max())
       return h,M
    return best_h,best_M
```
This can run up to starts+4 rounds. Each inner_dl uses fraction of remaining. However, if first local_search breaks early due no_progress before inner_dl, second gets more rem? It uses current rem, fractions based on rem not total. That's okay. But if first local_search uses full 0.55*rem, remaining 0.45; second 0.30*0.45=13.5%, etc. Sum less than 100%, leaving leftover; round after will get 0.12*remaining. Could run more rounds. Good.

Potential bug: `local_search_binary` returns tuple length 3, but earlier I planned return best_h,best_M,best_C. In optimize_n, `h2,M2,C2 = ...` okay.

Need if `starts` empty? We always append v_parent.

`values_to_binary` with jitter 1e-6 for v_prev binary no effect.

The `randomize_binary` on best_h maybe best_h is int32; returns int32. Good.

Potential issue: In `optimize_n`, after local_search returns best_h with same M but better score, best_score updated? local_search returned best_M and best_C; we compute sc2. Good.

`quick_threshold_scan` code with no lambda.

```
def quick_threshold_scan(parent, deadline, seed):
    c_by_n={}
    best_c=float('inf'); best_h=None; best_n=0
    tstart=time.time()
    max_n=400
    n=120
    while n <= max_n:
       if time.time() > deadline - 30: break
       h=values_to_binary(parent,n,None,0.0)
       C=compute_counts(h,n); M=int(np.max(C))
       c=2.0*M/float(n)
       old=c_by_n.get(n, float('inf'))
       if c < old: c_by_n[n]=c
       if c < best_c:
          best_c=c; best_h=h.copy(); best_n=n
       n += 2
    # top by c
    items=[]
    for nn,cc in c_by_n.items(): items.append((cc,nn))
    items.sort()
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed+31337)
    for k in range(min(6,len(items))):
       nn=int(items[k][1])
       if time.time() > deadline - 10: break
       for tr in range(6):
          jitter=1e-5*(tr+1)
          h=values_to_binary(parent,nn,rng,jitter)
          C=compute_counts(h,nn); M=int(np.max(C)); c=2.0*M/float(nn)
          old=c_by_n.get(nn,float('inf'))
          if c < old: c_by_n[nn]=c
          if c < best_c:
             best_c=c; best_h=h.copy(); best_n=nn
    return best_h,best_c,best_n,c_by_n
```
If deadline after t0 small, `-30` might be before start, loop breaks immediately. For budget 1100, okay.

Note: If `parent` length n and we call values_to_binary with rng None jitter 0, for m==n it returns vals.copy, sorted. Good.

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0=time.time()

Now, let's think about exact feasibility of returned binary: sum=h.sum() should be n/2. `values_to_binary` ensures. `local_search` swaps maintain sum. `randomize_binary` swaps maintain. `optimize_n` fallback values_to_binary. If best_h parent nonbinary, sum maybe 1/dx exactly if feasible; parent from harness should. If parent not exact due floating, maybe small error. We could project parent if it's the final best? But if binary found, no. If fallback parent, perhaps use `project_feasible`? Could implement a simple area adjust for continuous: ensure sum=n/2 exactly? For float, exact sum maybe tricky. But parent likely feasible. To be safe, if best_h not integer (any values non 0/1?), we can project:
```
def make_feasible(h,n):
   h=np.asarray(h,float).copy(); target=n/2.0; h=np.clip(h,0,1); diff=target-h.sum(); if diff>0: add to room... else subtract...
return h
```
But if binary, not needed. We can include a final if not all values close to 0/1? However if parent nonbinary best, maybe we should keep as is because changing could increase C. But evaluation likely based on provided parent feasible. The task says initial if available use directly; it likely feasible. We can skip.

Need if `best_h` is int32, `compute_c5_general` casts float. Good.

Now, let's test code snippets for syntax.

In `local_search_binary`, line `if M > best_M + 8:` M could be higher after perturbation; reset. If `best_M` was 10**9? no best initialized.

`add_candidate_pairs` uses `seen` set of int keys. If n large, `i*n+j` possible collision? Since 0≤j<n, unique. Good.

`choose_high_lags`: if len idxM > max_lags, `idxM = idxM[rng.choice(...)]` returns array. If len idxM < max_lags no. Then loop appending int(idx). Good. `idxM1` if M>1. If `idxM1` length > take sample. Loop append. If high length maybe > max_lags due idxM exactly max_lags plus idxM1? We only add up to rem; rem = max_lags-len(high) positive. Good.

`generate_candidates` uses `per_case=3`; limit maybe 300. Candidates maybe fewer. Good.

Potential performance: `high_score` called many times. `np.sum(C[C>=thr])` creates boolean vector. n small. Good.

Potential bug: In `local_search_binary`, after a plateau accept (M unchanged but score lower), `no_prog=0`; but `best_score` may not update until next top. okay.

```python
import numpy as np
import time


def compute_c5_general(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if n is None or int(n) != h.size:
        n = h.size
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    return float(np.max(c) * dx)


def interp_values(vals, n):
    vals = np.asarray(vals, dtype=float)
    m = vals.size
    if m == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if m == n:
        return vals.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, m)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n)
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, vals)


def values_to_binary(vals, n, rng=None, jitter=0.0):
    vals = np.asarray(vals, dtype=float)
    m = vals.size
    if m == 0:
        vals = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    elif m != n:
        x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, m)
        x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n)
        vals = np.interp(x_new, x_old, vals)

    if jitter > 0.0 and rng is not None:
        vals = vals + jitter * rng.randn(n)

    order = np.argsort(vals, kind="stable")
    target = n // 2
    b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int32)
    b[order[target:]] = 1

    s = int(b.sum())
    if s > target:
        idx = np.where(b == 1)[0]
        cnt = s - target
        for k in range(cnt):
            b[int(idx[k])] = 0
    elif s < target:
        idx = np.where(b == 0)[0]
        cnt = target - s
        for k in range(cnt):
            b[int(idx[k])] = 1
    return b


def compute_counts(h, n=None):
    h = np.asarray(h)
    if n is None:
        n = h.size
    a = h.astype(np.int32, copy=False)
    b = 1 - a
    c = np.correlate(a, b, mode="full")
    return np.asarray(c, dtype=np.int32)


def high_score(C, M, band=2):
    thr = M - band
    if thr < 0:
        thr = 0
    return int(np.sum(C[C >= thr]))


def add_unique(lst, x):
    for y in lst:
        if y == x:
            return
    lst.append(x)


def add_candidate_pairs(cand, seen, zlist, olist, ell, count, n, rng, avoid):
    lz = len(zlist)
    lo = len(olist)
    if lz == 0 or lo == 0:
        return
    added = 0
    max_attempts = count * 12 + 5
    attempts = 0
    while added < count and attempts < max_attempts:
        attempts += 1
        i = int(zlist[rng.randint(lz)])
        j = int(olist[rng.randint(lo)])
        if avoid and ((j - i) == ell or (j - i) == -ell):
            continue
        key = i * n + j
        if key not in seen:
            seen.add(key)
            cand.append((i, j))
            added += 1


def choose_high_lags(C, M, n, rng, max_lags):
    high = []
    idxM = np.where(C == M)[0]
    if len(idxM) > max_lags:
        idxM = idxM[rng.choice(len(idxM), max_lags, replace=False)]
    for idx in idxM:
        high.append(int(idx))

    rem = max_lags - len(high)
    if rem > 0 and M > 1:
        idxM1 = np.where(C == (M - 1))[0]
        if len(idxM1) > 0:
            take = min(rem, len(idxM1))
            if take < len(idxM1):
                idxM1 = idxM1[rng.choice(len(idxM1), take, replace=False)]
            for idx in idxM1:
                high.append(int(idx))

    if len(high) > 1:
        perm = rng.permutation(len(high))
        low = []
        for k in perm:
            low.append(high[int(k)])
        high = low
    return high


def generate_candidates(h, C, M, off, n, rng, limit):
    cand = []
    seen = set()
    max_lags = min(60, max(20, n // 5))
    high = choose_high_lags(C, M, n, rng, max_lags)
    zmask = (h == 0)
    omask = (h == 1)
    per_case = 3

    for idx in high:
        ell = int(idx - off)
        pos = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int8)
        neg = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int8)

        if ell >= 0:
            r = n - ell
            if r > 0:
                pos[:r] = 1 - h[ell:]
                neg[ell:] = h[:r]
        else:
            d = -ell
            r = n - d
            if r > 0:
                pos[d:] = 1 - h[:r]
                neg[:r] = h[d:]

        A = pos - neg
        B = -pos + neg

        zA_m1 = np.nonzero(zmask & (A == -1))[0]
        zA_0 = np.nonzero(zmask & (A == 0))[0]
        oB_0 = np.nonzero(omask & (B == 0))[0]
        oB_m1 = np.nonzero(omask & (B == -1))[0]

        add_candidate_pairs(cand, seen, zA_m1, oB_m1, ell, per_case, n, rng, False)
        add_candidate_pairs(cand, seen, zA_m1, oB_0, ell, per_case, n, rng, True)
        add_candidate_pairs(cand, seen, zA_0, oB_m1, ell, per_case, n, rng, True)

        if len(cand) >= limit:
            break

    zeros = np.nonzero(zmask)[0]
    ones = np.nonzero(omask)[0]
    if zeros.size and ones.size:
        need = min(limit - len(cand), max(60, n // 4))
        if need > 0:
            attempts = 0
            max_attempts = 5 * need
            while len(cand) < limit and attempts < max_attempts:
                attempts += 1
                i = int(zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))])
                j = int(ones[rng.randint(len(ones))])
                key = i * n + j
                if key not in seen:
                    seen.add(key)
                    cand.append((i, j))
    return cand


def swap_newC(C, h, omh, i, j, off, n):
    newC = C.copy()

    newC[off - i:off + n - i] += omh
    newC[off + i - n + 1:off + i + 1] -= h[::-1]
    newC[off - j:off + n - j] -= omh
    newC[off + j - n + 1:off + j + 1] += h[::-1]

    newC[off] -= 2
    newC[off + j - i] += 1
    newC[off + i - j] += 1

    return newC, int(newC.max())


def randomize_binary(h, n, k, rng):
    h = h.astype(np.int32, copy=True)
    for _ in range(k):
        z = np.nonzero(h == 0)[0]
        o = np.nonzero(h == 1)[0]
        if z.size == 0 or o.size == 0:
            break
        i = int(z[rng.randint(len(z))])
        j = int(o[rng.randint(len(o))])
        h[i], h[j] = 1, 0
    return h


def random_run_binary(n, seed, avg=3):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int32)
    pos = 0
    val = int(rng.randint(0, 2))
    low = max(1, avg - 2)
    high = avg + 2
    while pos < n:
        L = int(rng.randint(low, high + 1))
        end = pos + L
        if end > n:
            end = n
        h[pos:end] = val
        val = 1 - val
        pos = end

    target = n // 2
    s = int(h.sum())
    if s > target:
        idx = np.where(h == 1)[0]
        perm = rng.permutation(len(idx))
        cnt = s - target
        for k in range(cnt):
            h[int(idx[perm[k]])] = 0
    elif s < target:
        idx = np.where(h == 0)[0]
        perm = rng.permutation(len(idx))
        cnt = target - s
        for k in range(cnt):
            h[int(idx[perm[k]])] = 1
    return h


def local_search_binary(h, C, M, deadline, seed, limit=None):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    n = int(h.size)
    off = n - 1
    h = h.astype(np.int32, copy=True)
    C = C.astype(np.int32, copy=True)
    M = int(C.max())

    if limit is None:
        if n <= 200:
            limit = int(min(900, max(300, 3 * n)))
        else:
            limit = int(min(1200, max(500, 2 * n)))

    max_no_prog = 180 if n <= 200 else 80

    best_h = h.copy()
    best_C = C.copy()
    best_M = M
    best_score = high_score(C, M)
    no_prog = 0

    while time.time() < deadline:
        cur_score = high_score(C, M)
        if M < best_M or (M == best_M and cur_score < best_score):
            best_M = M
            best_score = cur_score
            best_h = h.copy()
            best_C = C.copy()

        old_score = cur_score
        old_countM = int(np.count_nonzero(C >= M))
        omh = 1 - h
        candidates = generate_candidates(h, C, M, off, n, rng, limit)
        improved = False

        for pair in candidates:
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            i = pair[0]
            j = pair[1]
            if h[i] != 0 or h[j] != 1:
                continue
            newC, newM = swap_newC(C, h, omh, i, j, off, n)
            acc = False
            if newM < M:
                acc = True
            elif newM == M:
                new_score = high_score(newC, M)
                if new_score < old_score:
                    acc = True
                else:
                    new_countM = int(np.count_nonzero(newC >= M))
                    if new_countM < old_countM:
                        acc = True
            if acc:
                C = newC
                M = newM
                h[i] = 1
                h[j] = 0
                improved = True
                no_prog = 0
                break

        if improved:
            continue

        no_prog += 1
        zeros = np.nonzero(h == 0)[0]
        ones = np.nonzero(h == 1)[0]
        if zeros.size and ones.size:
            ntrials = 80 + min(300, no_prog * 5)
            for _ in range(ntrials):
                if time.time() > deadline:
                    break
                i = int(zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))])
                j = int(ones[rng.randint(len(ones))])
                newC, newM = swap_newC(C, h, omh, i, j, off, n)
                acc = False
                if newM < M:
                    acc = True
                elif newM == M:
                    new_score = high_score(newC, M)
                    if new_score < old_score:
                        acc = True
                    else:
                        new_countM = int(np.count_nonzero(newC >= M))
                        if new_countM < old_countM:
                            acc = True
                else:
                    if no_prog > 20 and newM <= M + (1 if no_prog < 60 else 2):
                        p = 0.01 * (no_prog - 20)
                        if p > 0.15:
                            p = 0.15
                        if rng.random() < p:
                            acc = True
                if acc:
                    C = newC
                    M = newM
                    h[i] = 1
                    h[j] = 0
                    improved = True
                    no_prog = 0
                    break

        if improved:
            continue

        if no_prog > 20 and no_prog % 20 == 0:
            maxM = best_M + 5
            for _ in range(8):
                if time.time() > deadline:
                    break
                z = np.nonzero(h == 0)[0]
                o = np.nonzero(h == 1)[0]
                if z.size == 0 or o.size == 0:
                    break
                i = int(z[rng.randint(len(z))])
                j = int(o[rng.randint(len(o))])
                newC, newM = swap_newC(C, h, 1 - h, i, j, off, n)
                if newM <= maxM:
                    C = newC
                    M = newM
                    h[i] = 1
                    h[j] = 0
            no_prog = 0
            if M > best_M + 8:
                h = best_h.copy()
                C = best_C.copy()
                M = best_M

        if no_prog > max_no_prog:
            break

    cur_score = high_score(C, M)
    if M < best_M or (M == best_M and cur_score < best_score):
        best_M = M
        best_score = cur_score
        best_h = h.copy()
        best_C = C.copy()
    return best_h, best_M, best_C


def optimize_n(n, parent_vals, prev_h, prev_n, deadline, seed):
    t_enter = time.time()
    if deadline - t_enter < 5 or n < 2:
        return None, None
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    v_parent = interp_values(parent_vals, n)
    starts = []

    if prev_h is not None and len(prev_h) > 0:
        if int(prev_n) == int(n):
            v_prev = np.asarray(prev_h, dtype=float).copy()
            starts.append(v_prev)
            mix = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
            for i in range(n):
                mix[i] = 0.5 * v_prev[i] + 0.5 * v_parent[i]
            starts.append(mix)
        else:
            v_prev = interp_values(prev_h, n)
            mix = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
            for i in range(n):
                mix[i] = 0.5 * v_parent[i] + 0.5 * v_prev[i]
            starts.append(mix)
            starts.append(v_parent)
    else:
        starts.append(v_parent)

    if n <= 600:
        starts.append(random_run_binary(n, seed + 1234, 3).astype(float))
    if len(starts) > 4:
        starts = starts[:4]

    best_h = None
    best_M = 10 ** 9
    best_score = 10 ** 18
    initial_h = None
    max_rounds = len(starts) + 4

    for round_idx in range(max_rounds):
        if time.time() > deadline - 8:
            break

        if round_idx < len(starts):
            v = starts[round_idx]
            h = values_to_binary(v, n, rng, jitter=1e-6)
        else:
            if best_h is None:
                if initial_h is None:
                    v = starts[0] if len(starts) else v_parent
                    h = values_to_binary(v, n, rng, jitter=1e-6)
                else:
                    h = initial_h.copy()
            else:
                k = 5 + int(rng.randint(0, 25))
                if round_idx > 6:
                    k = max(3, k // 2)
                h = randomize_binary(best_h, n, k, rng)

        C = compute_counts(h, n)
        M = int(C.max())
        if initial_h is None:
            initial_h = h.copy()

        sc = high_score(C, M)
        if M < best_M or (M == best_M and sc < best_score):
            best_M = M
            best_score = sc
            best_h = h.copy()

        rem = deadline - time.time()
        if rem <= 5:
            break

        if round_idx == 0:
            frac = 0.55
        elif round_idx == 1:
            frac = 0.30
        elif round_idx == 2:
            frac = 0.15
        else:
            frac = 0.12

        inner_dl = time.time() + max(10.0, frac * rem)
        if inner_dl > deadline:
            inner_dl = deadline

        h2, M2, C2 = local_search_binary(h, C, M, inner_dl, seed * 1000 + round_idx)
        if h2 is not None:
            sc2 = high_score(C2, M2)
            if M2 < best_M or (M2 == best_M and sc2 < best_score):
                best_M = M2
                best_score = sc2
                best_h = h2.copy()

    if best_h is None:
        h = values_to_binary(starts[0] if len(starts) else v_parent, n, rng, jitter=0.0)
        C = compute_counts(h, n)
        M = int(C.max())
        return h, M
    return best_h, best_M


def quick_threshold_scan(parent, deadline, seed):
    c_by_n = {}
    best_c = float("inf")
    best_h = None
    best_n = 0

    n = 120
    while n <= 400:
        if time.time() > deadline - 30:
            break
        h = values_to_binary(parent, n, None, 0.0)
        C = compute_counts(h, n)
        M = int(np.max(C))
        c = 2.0 * M / float(n)
        old = c_by_n.get(n, float("inf"))
        if c < old:
            c_by_n[n] = c
        if c < best_c:
            best_c = c
            best_h = h.copy()
            best_n = n
        n += 2

    items = []
    for nn, cc in c_by_n.items():
        items.append((cc, nn))
    items.sort()

    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed + 31337)
    for k in range(min(6, len(items))):
        nn = int(items[k][1])
        if time.time() > deadline - 10:
            break
        for tr in range(6):
            jitter = 1e-5 * (tr + 1)
            h = values_to_binary(parent, nn, rng, jitter=jitter)
            C = compute_counts(h, nn)
            M = int(np.max(C))
            c = 2.0 * M / float(nn)
            old = c_by_n.get(nn, float("inf"))
            if c < old:
                c_by_n[nn] = c
            if c < best_c:
                best_c = c
                best_h = h.copy()
                best_n = nn

    return best_h, best_c, best_n, c_by_n


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    budget = float(budget_s)
    deadline = t0 + max(1.0, budget - 4.0)
    seed = int(seed)

    try:
        parent = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is None or parent.size == 0:
        parent = random_run_binary(142, seed, 3).astype(float)

    n_par = int(parent.size)
    best_h = parent.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5_general(best_h, n_par)
    best_n = n_par

    try:
        qh, qc, qn, c_by_n = quick_threshold_scan(parent, deadline, seed)
        if qh is not None and qc < best_c:
            best_c = qc
            best_h = qh.copy()
            best_n = qn
    except Exception:
        c_by_n = {}

    phase_ns = []
    if best_n % 2 == 0 and 80 <= best_n <= 500:
        add_unique(phase_ns, int(best_n))

    items = []
    for nn, cc in c_by_n.items():
        items.append((cc, nn))
    items.sort()
    for k in range(min(5, len(items))):
        add_unique(phase_ns, int(items[k][1]))

    for nn in [142, 158, 200, 310, 134, 128, 250]:
        add_unique(phase_ns, nn)

    clean_ns = []
    for nn in phase_ns:
        if nn % 2 == 0 and 80 <= nn <= 500:
            clean_ns.append(nn)
    phase_ns = clean_ns[:5]
    if not phase_ns:
        phase_ns = [142]

    fracs = [0.50, 0.22, 0.12, 0.09, 0.07][:len(phase_ns)]

    for idx, nn in enumerate(phase_ns):
        if time.time() > deadline - 20:
            break
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        if rem < 20:
            break
        phase_dl = time.time() + max(15.0, fracs[idx] * rem)
        if phase_dl > deadline:
            phase_dl = deadline
        try:
            h_new, M_new = optimize_n(nn, parent, best_h, best_n, phase_dl, seed * 97 + nn)
        except Exception:
            h_new = None
        if h_new is not None:
            c_new = compute_c5_general(h_new, nn)
            if c_new < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_c = c_new
                best_h = h_new.copy()
                best_n = nn

    if deadline - time.time() > 50:
        n_fin = best_n if (best_n % 2 == 0 and 80 <= best_n <= 500) else 142
        try:
            h_new, M_new = optimize_n(n_fin, parent, best_h, best_n, deadline - 3, seed + 987654)
        except Exception:
            h_new = None
        if h_new is not None:
            c_new = compute_c5_general(h_new, n_fin)
            if c_new < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_c = c_new
                best_h = h_new.copy()
                best_n = n_fin

    if best_h is None:
        n_fin = 142
        best_h = random_run_binary(n_fin, seed, 3)
        best_c = compute_c5_general(best_h, n_fin)
        best_n = n_fin

    try:
        best_c = compute_c5_general(best_h, best_n)
    except Exception:
        pass

    return best_h, float(best_c), int(best_n)
```
